This is only used   │\\\\n│                     when serving default policies. (default: ALOHA_SIM)      │\\\\n│ --default-prompt {None}|STR                                                  │\\\\n│                     If provided, will be used in case the \\\\\\\"prompt\\\\\\\" key is    │\\\\n│                     not present in the data, or if the model doesn't have a  │\\\\n│                     default prompt. (default: None)                          │\\\\n│ --port INT          Port to serve the policy on. (default: 8000)             │\\\\n│ --record, --no-record                                                        │\\\\n│                     Record the policy's behavior for debugging.
